root = Path(root).resolve()\n    errors, deferred = [], []\n    schema = read_json(root/'docs/parity/inventory.schema.json', None)\n    if schema is None:\n        return ['docs/parity/inventory.schema.json: missing contract'], []\n    errors += schema_errors(inventory, schema, 'inventory')\n    if errors:\n        return errors, []\n    # Fragments supply reviewed destinations without rewriting coordinator-owned inventory.\n    effective = {row['upstreamPath']: row for row in inventory}\n    fragment_owners = set()\n    for file in sorted((root/'docs/parity/adaptations').glob('*.json')):\n        fragment = read_json(file, [])\n        fragment_errors = schema_errors(fragment, schema, str(file.relative_to(root)))\n        errors += fragment_errors\n        if not fragment_errors:\n            for row in fragment:\n                source = row['upstreamPath']\n                if source not in effective:\n                    errors.append(f'{file.name}: unknown active fragment source {source}')\n                elif source in fragment_owners:\n                    errors.append(f'{file.name}: duplicate active fragment owner {source}')\n                elif any(v != effective[source].get(k) for k,v in row.items() if k != 'verification'):\n                    # During leaf development only a scoped pending row can be supplied.\n                    if scope == source and effective[source]['adaptationStatus'] == 'pending' and all(row[k] == effective[source][k] for k in ('upstreamCommit','upstreamSha256')):\n                        effective[source] = row\n                    else:\n                        errors.append(f'{file.name}: conflicting active fragment {source}')\n                fragment_owners.add(source)\n    rows = list(effective.values())\n    for row in rows:\n        for path in [row['destinationPath'], *row['dependencies']]:\n            if contained(root, path) is None:\n                errors.append(f'{row[\"upstreamPath\"]}: {path}: path or symlink escapes package')\n    selected = [row for row in rows if scope is None or row['upstreamPath'] == scope]\n    if scope is not None and not selected:\n        errors.append(f'{scope}: scope is not inventoried')\n    destinations = {row['destinationPath']: row for row in rows}\n    seen = set()\n    for row in inventory:\n        path = row['upstreamPath']\n        if path in seen:\n            errors.append(f'{path}: duplicate inventory source')\n        seen.add(path)\n        if contained(root, path) is None:\n            errors.append(f'{path}: source path escapes package')\n    def check(path, context, allow_deferred=True):\n        candidate = contained(root, path)\n        if candidate is not None and candidate.resolve().is_relative_to(root / '.upstream'):\n            errors.append(f'{context}: {path}: inert review data cannot be an operative destination or dependency')\n            return None\n        if candidate is None:\n            errors.append(f'{context}: {path}: path or symlink escapes package')\n        elif not candidate.exists():\n            row = destinations.get(path)\n            if allow_deferred and scope is not None and row and row['adaptationStatus'] == 'pending':\n                deferred.append(f'{context}: {path}: inventoried pending dependency')\n            else:\n                errors.append(f'{context}: {path}: missing path')\n        return candidate\n    for row in selected:\n        check(row['destinationPath'], row['upstreamPath'], allow_deferred=False)\n        for dependency in row['dependencies']:\n            # Explicit upstream dependencies map to their package destinations.\n            check(effective.get(dependency, {}).get('destinationPath', dependency), row['upstreamPath'])\n    names = {}\n    for file in sorted((root/'skills').glob('*/SKILL.md')):\n        relative = file.relative_to(root).as_posix()\n        check(relative, 'root discovery')\n        if contained(root, relative) is None:\n            continue\n        name = re.search(r'^name:\\s*(.+)$', file.read_text(), re.M)\n        if name:\n            key = name[1].strip(' \"\\'')\n            if key in names:\n                errors.append(f'{relative}: duplicate root skill {key}, also {names[key]}')\n            names[key] = relative\n        if file.parent.name == 'benny' or (name and name[1].strip() == 'benny'):\n            errors.append(f'{relative}: benny must stay outside registered root skills')\n    for file in sorted(root.rglob('SKILL.md')):\n        relative = file.relative_to(root).as_posix()\n        if relative.startswith(('.git/', '.superpowers/', '.upstream/')):\n            continue\n        if not re.fullmatch(r'skills/[^/]+/SKILL\\.md', relative):\n            errors.append(f'{relative}: recursive discovery entrypoint must be a registered root skill; use a reference filename for support instructions')\n    manifest = read_json(root/'plugin.json', None)\n    if not isinstance(manifest, dict):\n        errors.append('plugin.json: missing or invalid manifest')\n    else:\n        if 'skills' in manifest or 'skills' in manifest.get('extensions', {}).get('com.openai', {}):\n            errors.append('plugin.json: root skill discovery must be implicit')\n        interface = manifest.get('extensions', {}).get('com.openai', {}).get('interface', {})\n        for key in ('logo', 'icon'):\n            if key in interface:\n                check(interface[key], 'plugin.json')\n    dispositions = read_json(root/'docs/parity/remnant-dispositions.json', [])\n    if not isinstance(dispositions, list):\n        errors.append('docs/parity/remnant-dispositions.json: expected array')\n        dispositions = []\n    for entry in dispositions:\n        if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get('kind') == errors.append(f'docs/parity/remnant-dispositions.json: {entry.get(\"path\")}: historical exemption is limited to approved spec and plan')\n        if isinstance(entry, dict) and (not historical = any(entry.get('kind') == 'historical' for entry in approved)\n        if not historical:\n            # Fenced literals are not rendered links. mask_inline_code(operative)\n            links = re.findall(r'!?\\[[^\\]]*\\]\\(\\s*(<[^>]*>|[^\\s)]+)', operative)\n            definitions = re.findall(r'^\\s*\\[([^\\]]+)\\]:\\s*(<[^>]*>|\\S+)', operative, re.M)\n            links += [target for _, target in definitions]\n            labels = {label.casefold() for label, _ in definitions}\n            for label, reference in re.findall(r'!?\\[([^\\]]+)\\]\\[([^\\]]*)\\]', operative):\n                reference = reference or label\n                if reference.casefold() not in labels:\n                    errors.append(f'{relative}: [{reference}]: undefined Markdown reference')\n            for link in links:\n                target = unquote(link.strip('<>'))\n                parsed = Preserve the skill voice, validate structural contracts, and report unknown trigger performance without assuming create-skill is installed or PR writes are authorized.\",\n    \"requirements\": [\n      \"Inspect exposed capabilities and the available authoring workflow instructions and actual validator before use; identify the missing create-skill dependency.\",\n      \"Preserve voice ownership, decision-changing prose, confusing-rule explanations, scope-matched tone, structural-source pointers, path delegation and proposals for recurring uncaptured workflows.\",\n      \"Check name and description frontmatter, referenced files and cross-skill links; run structural test cases with fixtures and skip subjective tests.\",\n      \"Apply Opening a PR only when its dependency, capabilities and authorization permit; preserve the skill artifact when PR delivery is deferred.\",\n      \"Reply with skill summary, key design decisions and validation notes; distinguish observed results from unknown trigger performance.\"\n    ],\n    \"expectedFull\": \"An available supported authoring workflow runs after its instructions and validator are inspected. The authored skill passes the observed structural checks and structural test cases. Subjective tests are skipped. The reply identifies the actual workflow, states create-skill is absent unless discovered, leaves unknown trigger performance unverified and reports observed authorized PR delivery or its explicit deferral.\",\n    \"expectedReduced\": \"With authorized writes but no supported authoring workflow, produce only a provisional skill draft and manual checklist. Preserve the missing create-skill block, unexecuted validation and unknown trigger performance. If authoring and validation succeed but PR delivery is unavailable, retain the validated local skill and report the deferred PR dependency.\",\n    \"expectedBlocked\": \"Without an available authoring workflow and without authorization or capability for a provisional draft, report the missing create-skill dependency and unavailable operation. Unavailable validation or PR delivery remains explicitly blocked or deferred; no completion claim substitutes for it.\",\n    \"forbiddenClaims\": [\n      \"create-skill shipped or executed without discovery and observed evidence.\",\n      \"A provisional draft or manual checklist proves supported authoring execution or validator success.\",\n      \"Structural checks prove trigger performance, subjective quality or host behavioral parity.\",\n      \"A PR was created, commits made or external writes performed without authorization and observed results.\",\n      \"Path delegation to another skill proves independent worker execution.\"\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sourceFiles\": [\n    \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/authoring-a-skill.md\",\n    \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/opening-a-pr.md\",\n    \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/principle-encode-lessons-in-structure/SKILL.md\"\n  ]\n}\n"
